================================================================================
TAKEONE — PHASE 2 (STAGE 2) POST-AUDIT FIXES & REGRESSION TESTS
================================================================================

SUMMARY OF 10 PATCHED ISSUES (BUG-11 .. BUG-20):
  BUG-11 [HIGH] (StudioFragment.java): Sub-selection commitDspFx() no longer
    sets slot.enabled = false across dspConfig.slots or overwrites SharedPreferences.
    Tracks dspBakedToRaw = true and dspSubRangeBaked = true so whole-take Preview
    and Save never double-bake a processed selection, while selecting a second
    region previews and applies the active rack immediately without re-checking.
  BUG-12 [HIGH] (FxRackChain.java, DspChain.java, LegacyToneProcessor.java,
    StudioFragment.java): Independent WARMTH_BODY & VOCAL_PRESENCE ordering and
    single-slot Wet/Dry mix. Preserves the bit-identical combined pass when
    adjacent, splits execution at the exact downstream slot position when
    separated by another active processor, prevents a disabled tone slot from
    trapping its enabled sibling, and enables the Wet-Dry Mix control when only
    one tone slot is active.
  BUG-13 [MEDIUM] (DspChain.java): syncSlotsFromLegacyFields() preserves
    unchecked (enabled == false) slots in previousOrder with their value,
    wetDry, and extraParams intact instead of dropping them on manual/mode sync.
  BUG-14 [MEDIUM] (StudioFragment.java): setFxPanelVisible(true) only auto-seeds
    VOICE_POLISH on initial default state, never overwriting an intentional
    CLEAR_ALL ("Flat / No Effects") or custom unchecked rack.
  BUG-15 [MEDIUM] (StudioFragment.java): formatSlotSubtitle() appends
    " • Mix XX%" when wetDry < 1.0f and summarizes non-default extraParams.
  BUG-16 [MEDIUM] (FxRackChain.java, StudioFragment.java): Added cached
    FxRackChain.getPrototypeProcessor(id) so rebuildFxRackList() and
    formatSlotSubtitle() never allocate throwaway DSP instances on the UI thread.
  BUG-17 [MEDIUM] (LegacyToneProcessor.java, LegacyNoiseProcessor.java):
    prepare(sampleRate, channels) preserves exact 48 kHz crossover alpha literals
    at DspChain.SAMPLE_RATE (100% bit parity) and recomputes 1 - exp(-2*pi*fc/sr)
    at non-48 kHz sample rates.
  BUG-18 [LOW] (TeleValue.java): Fixed-step branch of steppedValues() appends
    max when (max - first) % step != 0, matching FxParameter.clampRaw().
  BUG-19 [LOW] (FxParameter.java): formatDisplay() for Scale.DECIBEL uses
    "%+.2f" when scaleDivisor >= 100 and (Math.abs(currentRaw) % 10) != 0 so
    values like -0.05 dB do not round to -0.1 dB.
  BUG-20 [LOW] (StudioFragment.java): Removed duplicate outdated Javadoc block
    above commitDspFx() and documented both selection and whole-take commits.

+    /**
+     * Processes only the 180 Hz Warmth stage when Warmth and Presence are
+     * separated by another active processor in the user's rack order.
+     */
+    void processWarmthOnly(float[] buf, int frames, int channels, float mix) {
+        if (buf == null || frames <= 0 || mix <= 0.0f) return;
+        final int ch = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, channels));
+        final float wAmt = warmth01();
+        if (wAmt <= 0.001f) return;
+        final float wAlpha = this.warmAlpha;
+        final float warmGain = wAmt * 1.45f * Math.min(1.0f, mix);
+        int offset = 0;
+        while (offset < frames) {
+            int blk = Math.min(DECIM, frames - offset);
+            for (int f = 0; f < blk; f++) {
+                int idx = (offset + f) * ch;
+                for (int c = 0; c < ch; c++) {
+                    float x = buf[idx + c];
+                    warmLp[c] += wAlpha * (x - warmLp[c]);
+                    buf[idx + c] = x + warmGain * warmLp[c];
+                }
+            }
+            offset += blk;
+        }
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Processes only the 3.5 kHz Vocal Presence stage when Warmth and Presence
+     * are separated by another active processor in the user's rack order.
+     */
+    void processPresenceOnly(float[] buf, int frames, int channels, float mix) {
+        if (buf == null || frames <= 0 || mix <= 0.0f) return;
+        final int ch = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, channels));
+        final float pAmt = presence01();
+        if (pAmt <= 0.001f) return;
+        final float pAlpha = this.presAlpha;
+        final float presGain = pAmt * 1.65f * Math.min(1.0f, mix);
+        int offset = 0;
+        while (offset < frames) {
+            int blk = Math.min(DECIM, frames - offset);
+            for (int f = 0; f < blk; f++) {
+                int idx = (offset + f) * ch;
+                for (int c = 0; c < ch; c++) {
+                    float x = buf[idx + c];
+                    presLp[c] += pAlpha * (x - presLp[c]);
+                    float hi = x - presLp[c];
+                    buf[idx + c] = x + presGain * hi;
+                }
+            }
+            offset += blk;
+        }
+    }
+

+    static String formatSlotSubtitle(DspChain.EffectSlot slot) {
+        return formatSlotSubtitle(slot, null);
+    }
+
+    static String formatSlotSubtitle(DspChain.EffectSlot slot,
+                                     DspChain.Config cfg) {
+        if (slot == null || slot.type == null) return "";
+        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
+        sb.append(slot.enabled ? "Enabled • " : "Bypassed • ");
+        sb.append(slot.type.paramLabel).append(": ")
+                .append(slot.value).append(slot.type.unit);
+        boolean isTone = slot.type == DspChain.EffectType.WARMTH_BODY
+                || slot.type == DspChain.EffectType.VOCAL_PRESENCE;
+        boolean mixActive = !isTone || cfg == null
+                || !cfg.hasBothToneSlotsEnabled();
+        int mixPct = Math.round(slot.wetDry * 100f);
+        if (mixActive && mixPct < 100) {
+            sb.append(" • Mix ").append(mixPct).append("%");
+        }
+        if (!slot.extraParams.isEmpty()) {
+            AudioProcessor meta =
+                    FxRackChain.getPrototypeProcessor(slot.type.id);
+            if (meta != null) {
+                for (FxParameter param : meta.getParameters()) {
+                    if (isPrimaryFxParam(param.id)
+                            || param.type == FxParameter.Type.ACTION) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    Integer raw = slot.extraParams.get(param.id);
+                    if (raw != null && raw != param.defaultRaw) {
+                        param.setCurrentRaw(raw);
+                        sb.append(" • ").append(param.name).append(": ")
+                                .append(param.formatDisplay());
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        sb.append(slot.expanded ? "  ▴" : "  ▾");
+        return sb.toString();
     }

+    @Test
+    public void singleToneSlotSupportsWetDryMixWhileDualToneStaysFullWet() {
+        DspChain.Config cfg = new DspChain.Config();
+        cfg.applyPreset(DspChain.Preset.CRISP_CLARITY);
+        assertFalse(cfg.hasBothToneSlotsEnabled());
+        for (DspChain.EffectSlot s : cfg.slots) {
+            if (s.type == DspChain.EffectType.VOCAL_PRESENCE) {
+                s.wetDry = 0.45f;
+            }
+        }
+        DspChain chain = new DspChain(2, cfg);
+        AudioProcessor toneProc = chain.getRack().getProcessor(
+                chain.getRack().indexOf("tone"));
+        assertEquals(0.45f, toneProc.getWetDryMix(), 0.0f);
+
+        // Enabling Warmth alongside Presence switches to the combined pass (1.0f)
+        cfg.addOrFocusSlot(DspChain.EffectType.WARMTH_BODY);
+        assertTrue(cfg.hasBothToneSlotsEnabled());
+        chain.updateConfig(cfg);
+        assertEquals(1.0f, toneProc.getWetDryMix(), 0.0f);
+    }

+    @Test
+    public void separatingWarmthAndPresenceAroundCompressorChangesAudioOutput() {
+        // Order 1: [WARMTH, PRESENCE, COMPRESSOR] (both before Compressor)
+        DspChain.Config cfg1 = new DspChain.Config();
+        cfg1.applyPreset(DspChain.Preset.CLEAR_ALL);
+        DspChain.EffectSlot w1 = cfg1.addOrFocusSlot(DspChain.EffectType.WARMTH_BODY);
+        DspChain.EffectSlot p1 = cfg1.addOrFocusSlot(DspChain.EffectType.VOCAL_PRESENCE);
+        DspChain.EffectSlot c1 = cfg1.addOrFocusSlot(DspChain.EffectType.COMPRESSOR);
+        w1.value = 85;
+        p1.value = 85;
+        c1.value = 85;
+        cfg1.syncLegacyFieldsFromSlots();
+
+        // Order 2: [WARMTH, COMPRESSOR, PRESENCE] (Presence moved after Compressor)
+        DspChain.Config cfg2 = new DspChain.Config();
+        cfg2.applyPreset(DspChain.Preset.CLEAR_ALL);
+        DspChain.EffectSlot w2 = cfg2.addOrFocusSlot(DspChain.EffectType.WARMTH_BODY);
+        DspChain.EffectSlot c2 = cfg2.addOrFocusSlot(DspChain.EffectType.COMPRESSOR);
+        DspChain.EffectSlot p2 = cfg2.addOrFocusSlot(DspChain.EffectType.VOCAL_PRESENCE);
+        w2.value = 85;
+        p2.value = 85;
+        c2.value = 85;
+        cfg2.syncLegacyFieldsFromSlots();
+
+        float[] sig1 = tone(1024, 1, 0.55f, 0.06f);
+        for (int i = 0; i < sig1.length; i += 5) {
+            sig1[i] += 0.35f * (float) Math.sin(i * 0.65);
+        }
+        float[] sig2 = sig1.clone();
+
+        new DspChain(1, cfg1).processInPlace(sig1, 1024);
+        new DspChain(1, cfg2).processInPlace(sig2, 1024);
+
+        boolean diff = false;
+        for (int i = 0; i < sig1.length; i++) {
+            if (Math.abs(sig1[i] - sig2[i]) > 1e-4f) {
+                diff = true;
+                break;
+            }
+        }
+        assertTrue("Moving Presence after Compressor had no effect on audio", diff);
+    }

+    @Test
+    public void syncSlotsFromLegacyFieldsPreservesDisabledSlotsAndTheirSettings() {
+        DspChain.Config cfg = new DspChain.Config();
+        cfg.applyPreset(DspChain.Preset.PODCAST_BROADCAST);
+        DspChain.EffectSlot gateSlot = null;
+        for (DspChain.EffectSlot s : cfg.slots) {
+            if (s.type == DspChain.EffectType.ROOM_GATE) gateSlot = s;
+        }
+        assertNotNull(gateSlot);
+        gateSlot.enabled = false;
+        gateSlot.value = 65;
+        gateSlot.wetDry = 0.4f;
+        gateSlot.extraParams.put("hold_ms", 120);
+        cfg.syncLegacyFieldsFromSlots();
+
+        cfg.noisePct = 70;
+        cfg.onManualAdjustment();
+
+        DspChain.EffectSlot preserved = null;
+        for (DspChain.EffectSlot s : cfg.slots) {
+            if (s.type == DspChain.EffectType.ROOM_GATE) preserved = s;
+        }
+        assertNotNull("Disabled ROOM_GATE slot was dropped by syncSlotsFromLegacyFields",
+                preserved);
+        assertFalse(preserved.enabled);
+        assertEquals(65, preserved.value);
+        assertEquals(0.4f, preserved.wetDry, 0.0f);
+        assertEquals(Integer.valueOf(120), preserved.extraParams.get("hold_ms"));
+    }

+    @Test
+    public void setSampleRateUpdatesToneAndNoiseCrossoversWhilePreserving48kBitParity() {
+        FxRackChain tone48 = new FxRackChain(1);
+        LegacyToneProcessor tp48 = new LegacyToneProcessor();
+        tp48.setLegacyAmounts(70, 70);
+        tone48.addProcessor(tp48);
+        tone48.setSampleRate(48000);
+
+        FxRackChain tone96 = new FxRackChain(1);
+        LegacyToneProcessor tp96 = new LegacyToneProcessor();
+        tp96.setLegacyAmounts(70, 70);
+        tone96.addProcessor(tp96);
+        tone96.setSampleRate(96000);
+
+        float[] tA = tone(256, 1, 0.6f, 0.04f);
+        float[] tB = tA.clone();
+        tone48.processInPlace(tA, 256);
+        tone96.processInPlace(tB, 256);
+        boolean toneDiff = false;
+        for (int i = 0; i < tA.length; i++) {
+            if (Math.abs(tA[i] - tB[i]) > 1e-4f) {
+                toneDiff = true;
+                break;
+            }
+        }
+        assertTrue("LegacyToneProcessor ignored sample rate change", toneDiff);
+
+        FxRackChain noise48 = new FxRackChain(1);
+        LegacyNoiseProcessor np48 = new LegacyNoiseProcessor();
+        np48.setLegacyAmount(80);
+        noise48.addProcessor(np48);
+        noise48.setSampleRate(48000);
+
+        FxRackChain noise96 = new FxRackChain(1);
+        LegacyNoiseProcessor np96 = new LegacyNoiseProcessor();
+        np96.setLegacyAmount(80);
+        noise96.addProcessor(np96);
+        noise96.setSampleRate(96000);
+
+        float[] nA = tone(256, 1, 0.6f, 0.04f);
+        float[] nB = nA.clone();
+        noise48.processInPlace(nA, 256);
+        noise96.processInPlace(nB, 256);
+        boolean noiseDiff = false;
+        for (int i = 0; i < nA.length; i++) {
+            if (Math.abs(nA[i] - nB[i]) > 1e-4f) {
+                noiseDiff = true;
+                break;
+            }
+        }
+        assertTrue("LegacyNoiseProcessor ignored sample rate change", noiseDiff);
+    }

+    public void decibelParameterFormatDisplayPreservesHundredthsPrecision() {

+        db.setCurrentRaw(125);
+        assertEquals("+1.25 dB", db.formatDisplay());
+    }
